SIR, I.&mdash;In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Tengyueh for the first quarter of the year 1906. II. REPORTS TO THE CUSTOMS Pack-animals with Loads. January. February. March. Total. Import 5,164 3,848 5,24o 14,252 Export 1,910 1,294 1,575 4,779 Transit 2,376 2,190 2,896 7,462 Total 9,450 7,332 9,711 26,493 Same Quarter, 1905 7,418 6,803 8,397 22,618 Note.&mdash;Loads are occasionally carried by men; in such cases, for the purposes of the above Table, four men are entered as equal to one pack-animal.
